00549 | function CashCountForm({
00550 |   title,
00551 |   description,
00552 |   quantities,
00553 |   onQuantityChange,
00554 |   disabled = false,
00555 | }: {
00556 |   title: string;
00557 |   description: string;
00558 |   quantities: CashCountQuantities;
00559 |   onQuantityChange: (denomination: number, value: string) => void;
00560 |   disabled?: boolean;
00561 | }) {
00562 |   const groups = [
00563 |     {
00564 |       title: "Billetes",
00565 |       denominations: CASH_DENOMINATIONS.filter(
00566 |         (denomination) => denomination >= 1000,
00567 |       ),
00568 |     },
00569 |     {
00570 |       title: "Monedas",
00571 |       denominations: CASH_DENOMINATIONS.filter(
00572 |         (denomination) => denomination < 1000,
00573 |       ),
00574 |     },
00575 |   ];
00576 | 
00577 |   return (
00578 |     <div>
00579 |       <div className="mb-3">
00580 |         <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-800">{title}</p>
00581 | 
00582 |         <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-neutral-500">{description}</p>
00583 |       </div>
00584 | 
00585 |       <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
00586 |         {groups.map((group) => (
00587 |           <div
00588 |             key={group.title}
00589 |             className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white"
00590 |           >
00591 |             <div className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 px-3 py-2">
00592 |               <p className="text-xs font-bold text-neutral-800">
00593 |                 {group.title}
00594 |               </p>
00595 |             </div>
00596 | 
00597 |             <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_76px_1fr] gap-2 border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50/60 px-3 py-1.5">
00598 |               <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-neutral-500">
00599 |                 Denominación
00600 |               </p>
00601 | 
00602 |               <p className="text-center text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-neutral-500">
00603 |                 Cantidad
00604 |               </p>
00605 | 
00606 |               <p className="text-right text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-neutral-500">
00607 |                 Subtotal
00608 |               </p>
00609 |             </div>
00610 | 
00611 |             {group.denominations.map((denomination) => {
00612 |               const rawQuantity = quantities[denomination] || "";
00613 |               const parsedQuantity =
00614 |                 rawQuantity === "" ? 0 : Number(rawQuantity);
00615 | 
00616 |               const subtotal =
00617 |                 Number.isInteger(parsedQuantity) && parsedQuantity >= 0
00618 |                   ? denomination * parsedQuantity
00619 |                   : 0;
00620 | 
00621 |               return (
00622 |                 <div
00623 |                   key={denomination}
00624 |                   className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_76px_1fr] items-center gap-2 border-b border-neutral-100 px-3 py-1.5 last:border-b-0"
00625 |                 >
00626 |                   <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800">
00627 |                     {formatCurrency(denomination)}
00628 |                   </p>
00629 | 
00630 |                   <input
00631 |                     type="text"
00632 |                     inputMode="numeric"
00633 |                     pattern="[0-9]*"
00634 |                     value={rawQuantity}
00635 |                     onChange={(event) =>
00636 |                       onQuantityChange(denomination, event.target.value)
00637 |                     }
00638 |                     placeholder="0"
00639 |                     disabled={disabled}
00640 |                     aria-label={`Cantidad de ${formatCurrency(denomination)}`}
00641 |                     className="h-8 w-full rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 bg-white px-2 text-center text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-900 outline-none transition focus:border-violet-400 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-violet-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-neutral-100"
00642 |                   />
00643 | 
00644 |                   <p className="text-right text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700">
00645 |                     {formatCurrency(subtotal)}
00646 |                   </p>
00647 |                 </div>
00648 |               );
00649 |             })}
00650 |           </div>
00651 |         ))}
00652 |       </div>
00653 |     </div>
00654 |   );
00655 | }

00734 |     [openingCashCount],
00735 |   );
00736 | 
00737 |   const closingCashTotal = useMemo(
00738 |     () => calculateCashCountTotal(closingCashCount),
00739 |     [closingCashCount],
00740 |   );
00741 |

00743 |     if (!closingDetail) {
00744 |       return 0;
00745 |     }
00746 | 
00747 |     return closingDetail.cashSales.reduce(
00748 |       (total, sale) => total + sale.total,
00749 |       0,
00750 |     );
00751 |   }, [closingDetail]);

00780 |     closingDetailCashSalesTotal === closingDetail.summary.cashSalesAmount;

01035 |   function updateCashCountQuantity(
01036 |     type: "OPENING" | "CLOSING",
01037 |     denomination: number,
01038 |     value: string,
01039 |   ) {
01040 |     if (value !== "" && !/^\d+$/.test(value)) {
01041 |       return;
01042 |     }
01043 | 
01044 |     if (type === "OPENING") {
01045 |       setOpeningCashCount((current) => ({
01046 |         ...current,
01047 |         [denomination]: value,
01048 |       }));
01049 | 
01050 |       return;
01051 |     }
01052 | 
01053 |     setClosingCashCount((current) => ({
01054 |       ...current,
01055 |       [denomination]: value,
01056 |     }));
01057 | 
01058 |     if (closingPreview) {
01059 |       setClosingPreview(null);
01060 |     }
01061 |   }

01090 |   async function previewClosing(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
01091 |     event.preventDefault();
01092 | 
01093 |     if (!session) {
01094 |       setMessageType("error");
01095 |       setMessage("No existe una caja abierta para cerrar.");
01096 |       return;
01097 |     }
01098 | 
01099 |     if (!isValidCashCount(closingCashCount)) {
01100 |       setMessageType("error");
01101 |       setMessage(
01102 |         "Las cantidades por denominación deben ser números enteros mayores o iguales a cero.",
01103 |       );
01104 |       return;
01105 |     }
01106 | 
01107 |     const cashCount = cashCountToEntries(closingCashCount);
01108 | 
01109 |     setLoadingClosingPreview(true);
01110 |     setMessage("");
01111 | 
01112 |     try {
01113 |       const response = await fetch("/api/operacion/caja/cierre/preview", {
01114 |         method: "POST",
01115 |         headers: {
01116 |           "Content-Type": "application/json",
01117 |         },
01118 |         body: JSON.stringify({
01119 |           cashCount,
01120 |         }),
01121 |       });
01122 | 
01123 |       const data = (await response.json()) as CashClosingPreviewResponse;
01124 | 
01125 |       if (!response.ok || !data.ok || !data.preview) {
01126 |         setMessageType("error");
01127 |         setMessage(
01128 |           data.message ||
01129 |             "No fue posible calcular la previsualización del cierre.",
01130 |         );
01131 |         return;
01132 |       }
01133 | 
01134 |       if (data.preview.cashRegisterSessionId !== session.id) {
01135 |         setMessageType("error");
01136 |         setMessage(
01137 |           "La sesión de caja cambió mientras se calculaba el cierre. Actualiza la página e inténtalo nuevamente.",
01138 |         );
01139 |         return;
01140 |       }
01141 | 
01142 |       setClosingPreview(data.preview);
01143 |       setMessage("");
01144 |     } catch (error) {
01145 |       console.error("Error calculando previsualización del cierre:", error);
01146 | 
01147 |       setMessageType("error");
01148 |       setMessage("Ocurrió un error inesperado al calcular el cierre de caja.");
01149 |     } finally {
01150 |       setLoadingClosingPreview(false);
01151 |     }
01152 |   }
01153 | 
01154 |   async function confirmClosing() {
01155 |     if (!session) {
01156 |       setMessageType("error");
01157 |       setMessage("No existe una caja abierta para cerrar.");
01158 |       return;
01159 |     }
01160 | 
01161 |     if (!closingPreview) {
01162 |       setMessageType("error");
01163 |       setMessage("Debes revisar el conteo antes de confirmar el cierre.");
01164 |       return;
01165 |     }
01166 | 
01167 |     const normalizedNotes = closingNotes.trim();
01168 | 
01169 |     if (closingPreview.requiresNotes && !normalizedNotes) {
01170 |       setMessageType("error");
01171 |       setMessage(
01172 |         "Debes registrar una observación cuando exista una diferencia de caja.",
01173 |       );
01174 |       return;
01175 |     }
01176 | 
01177 |     if (!isValidCashCount(closingCashCount)) {
01178 |       setMessageType("error");
01179 |       setMessage("La composición del efectivo contado no es válida.");
01180 |       return;
01181 |     }
01182 | 
01183 |     const cashCount = cashCountToEntries(closingCashCount);
01184 | 
01185 |     if (
01186 |       calculateCashCountTotal(closingCashCount) !==
01187 |       closingPreview.countedCashAmount
01188 |     ) {
01189 |       setClosingPreview(null);
01190 |       setMessageType("error");
01191 |       setMessage(
01192 |         "El conteo cambió después de revisar el cierre. Debes revisarlo nuevamente.",
01193 |       );
01194 |       return;
01195 |     }
01196 | 
01197 |     setSubmittingClosing(true);
01198 |     setMessage("");
01199 | 
01200 |     try {
01201 |       const response = await fetch("/api/operacion/caja/cierre", {
01202 |         method: "POST",
01203 |         headers: {
01204 |           "Content-Type": "application/json",
01205 |         },
01206 |         body: JSON.stringify({
01207 |           cashCount,
01208 |           closingNotes: normalizedNotes,
01209 |         }),
01210 |       });
01211 | 
01212 |       const data = (await response.json()) as CashClosingResponse;
01213 | 
01214 |       if (!response.ok || !data.ok || !data.closing) {
01215 |         setMessageType("error");
01216 |         setMessage(data.message || "No fue posible cerrar la caja.");
01217 |         return;
01218 |       }
01219 | 
01220 |       const completedClosingResult = data.closing;
01221 | 
01222 |       setSession(null);
01223 |       setMovements([]);
01224 |       setMovementTotals({
01225 |         cashIn: 0,
01226 |         cashOut: 0,
01227 |         net: 0,
01228 |       });
01229 |       resetClosingState();
01230 | 
01231 |       await loadCashRegister();
01232 | 
01233 |       setCompletedClosing(completedClosingResult);
01234 | 
01235 |       setMessageType("success");
01236 |       setMessage(data.message || "Caja cerrada correctamente.");
01237 |     } catch (error) {
01238 |       console.error("Error cerrando caja:", error);
01239 | 
01240 |       setMessageType("error");
01241 |       setMessage("Ocurrió un error inesperado al cerrar la caja.");
01242 |     } finally {
01243 |       setSubmittingClosing(false);
01244 |     }
01245 |   }
01246 |

01265 |   async function openCashRegister(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
01266 |     event.preventDefault();
01267 | 
01268 |     if (!isValidCashCount(openingCashCount)) {
01269 |       setMessageType("error");
01270 |       setMessage(
01271 |         "Las cantidades por denominación deben ser números enteros mayores o iguales a cero.",
01272 |       );
01273 |       return;
01274 |     }
01275 | 
01276 |     const cashCount = cashCountToEntries(openingCashCount);
01277 | 
01278 |     try {
01279 |       setSubmitting(true);
01280 |       setMessage("");
01281 | 
01282 |       const response = await fetch("/api/operacion/caja", {
01283 |         method: "POST",
01284 |         headers: {
01285 |           "Content-Type": "application/json",
01286 |         },
01287 |         body: JSON.stringify({
01288 |           cashCount,
01289 |           openingNotes: openingNotes.trim(),
01290 |         }),
01291 |       });
01292 | 
01293 |       const data = (await response.json()) as CashRegisterResponse;
01294 | 
01295 |       if (!response.ok || !data.ok) {
01296 |         if (data.session) {
01297 |           setSession(data.session);
01298 |         }
01299 | 
01300 |         setMessageType("error");
01301 |         setMessage(data.message || "No fue posible abrir la caja.");
01302 |         return;
01303 |       }
01304 | 
01305 |       setSession(data.session ?? null);
01306 |       setOpeningCashCount(createEmptyCashCount());
01307 |       setOpeningNotes("");
01308 |       setCompletedClosing(null);
01309 |       setMovements([]);
01310 |       setMovementTotals({
01311 |         cashIn: 0,
01312 |         cashOut: 0,
01313 |         net: 0,
01314 |       });
01315 | 
01316 |       setMessageType("success");
01317 |       setMessage(data.message || "Caja abierta correctamente.");
01318 | 
01319 |       await loadMovements();
01320 |     } catch (error) {
01321 |       console.error("Error abriendo caja:", error);
01322 | 
01323 |       setMessageType("error");
01324 |       setMessage("Ocurrió un error inesperado al abrir la caja.");
01325 |     } finally {
01326 |       setSubmitting(false);
01327 |     }
01328 |   }

01330 |   async function registerMovement(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
01331 |     event.preventDefault();
01332 | 
01333 |     if (!session) {
01334 |       setMessageType("error");
01335 |       setMessage("Debes abrir la caja antes de registrar movimientos.");
01336 |       return;
01337 |     }
01338 | 
01339 |     const normalizedAmount = movementAmount.trim();
01340 |     const parsedAmount = Number(normalizedAmount);
01341 |     const normalizedNotes = movementNotes.trim();
01342 | 
01343 |     if (
01344 |       normalizedAmount === "" ||
01345 |       !Number.isInteger(parsedAmount) ||
01346 |       parsedAmount <= 0
01347 |     ) {
01348 |       setMessageType("error");
01349 |       setMessage("El monto debe ser un número entero mayor que cero.");
01350 |       return;
01351 |     }
01352 | 
01353 |     if (movementReason === "OTHER" && !normalizedNotes) {
01354 |       setMessageType("error");
01355 |       setMessage("Debes explicar el motivo del movimiento.");
01356 |       return;
01357 |     }
01358 | 
01359 |     try {
01360 |       setSubmittingMovement(true);
01361 |       setMessage("");
01362 | 
01363 |       const response = await fetch("/api/operacion/caja/movimientos", {
01364 |         method: "POST",
01365 |         headers: {
01366 |           "Content-Type": "application/json",
01367 |         },
01368 |         body: JSON.stringify({
01369 |           movementType,
01370 |           amount: parsedAmount,
01371 |           reason: movementReason,
01372 |           notes: normalizedNotes,
01373 |         }),
01374 |       });
01375 | 
01376 |       const data = (await response.json()) as CashMovementsResponse;
01377 | 
01378 |       if (!response.ok || !data.ok) {
01379 |         setMessageType("error");
01380 |         setMessage(
01381 |           data.message || "No fue posible registrar el movimiento de caja.",
01382 |         );
01383 |         return;
01384 |       }
01385 | 
01386 |       setMovementAmount("");
01387 |       setMovementNotes("");
01388 |       setMovementReason(getDefaultReason(movementType));
01389 |       setShowMovementForm(false);
01390 | 
01391 |       setMessageType("success");
01392 |       setMessage(data.message || "Movimiento registrado correctamente.");
01393 | 
01394 |       await loadMovements();
01395 |     } catch (error) {
01396 |       console.error("Error registrando movimiento:", error);
01397 | 
01398 |       setMessageType("error");
01399 |       setMessage("Ocurrió un error inesperado al registrar el movimiento.");
01400 |     } finally {
01401 |       setSubmittingMovement(false);
01402 |     }
01403 |   }
01404 | 
01405 |   return (
01406 |     <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#F6F3FF] p-3">
01407 |       <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[calc(100vh-24px)] w-full max-w-[1600px] flex-col">
01408 |         <header className="flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-3 rounded-xl bg-white px-4 py-3 shadow-sm sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
01409 |           <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
01410 |             <Link
01411 |               href="/operacion"
01412 |               className="shrink-0 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-[12px] font-bold text-neutral-700 transition hover:border-violet-300 hover:bg-violet-50"
01413 |             >
01414 |               ← Operación
01415 |             </Link>

01428 |           <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">

00457 |               </>

00075 | function formatearFecha(fecha?: string | null) {
00076 |   if (!fecha) return "-";
00077 |   const d = new Date(fecha);
00078 |   if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return "-";
00079 |   return d.toLocaleDateString("es-CL");

00081 | 
00082 | function formatearFechaHora(fecha?: string | null) {
00083 |   if (!fecha) return "-";

00085 |   if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return "-";
00086 |   return d.toLocaleString("es-CL");

00089 | function formatearMes(mes: string) {
00090 |   const [year, month] = mes.split("-");
00091 |   const d = new Date(Number(year), Number(month) - 1, 1);
00092 |   return d.toLocaleDateString("es-CL", {
00093 |     month: "short",
00094 |     year: "numeric",
00095 |   });
00096 | }

00143 | function LineChart({
00144 |   title,
00145 |   labels,
00146 |   values,
00147 | }: {
00148 |   title: string;
00149 |   labels: string[];
00150 |   values: number[];
00151 | }) {
00152 |   const { points, maxValue } = useMemo(() => {
00153 |     const width = 680;
00154 |     const height = 220;
00155 |     const padding = 28;
00156 |     const max = Math.max(...values, 1);
00157 | 
00158 |     const pts = values.map((value, index) => {
00159 |       const x =
00160 |         values.length === 1
00161 |           ? width / 2
00162 |           : padding +
00163 |             ((width - padding * 2) * index) / Math.max(values.length - 1, 1);
00164 | 
00165 |       const y =
00166 |         height - padding - ((height - padding * 2) * value) / Math.max(max, 1);
00167 | 
00168 |       return `${x},${y}`;
00169 |     });
00170 | 
00171 |     return {
00172 |       points: pts.join(" "),
00173 |       maxValue: max,
00174 |     };
00175 |   }, [values]);
00176 | 
00177 |   if (values.length === 0) {
00178 |     return <EmptyState text={`No hay datos para ${title.toLowerCase()}.`} />;
00179 |   }
00180 | 
00181 |   return (
00182 |     <div className="rounded-2xl border border-violet-100 bg-white p-4">
00183 |       <p className="mb-4 text-sm font-semibold text-violet-700">{title}</p>
00184 | 
00185 |       <div className="overflow-x-auto">
00186 |         <div className="min-w-[720px]">
00187 |           <svg viewBox="0 0 680 220" className="h-[220px] w-full">
00188 |             <line
00189 |               x1="28"
00190 |               y1="192"
00191 |               x2="652"
00192 |               y2="192"
00193 |               stroke="#E5E7EB"
00194 |               strokeWidth="1"
00195 |             />
00196 |             <line
00197 |               x1="28"
00198 |               y1="28"
00199 |               x2="28"
00200 |               y2="192"
00201 |               stroke="#E5E7EB"
00202 |               strokeWidth="1"
00203 |             />
00204 | 
00205 |             <polyline
00206 |               fill="none"
00207 |               stroke="#7C3AED"
00208 |               strokeWidth="3"
00209 |               points={points}
00210 |               strokeLinecap="round"
00211 |               strokeLinejoin="round"
00212 |             />
00213 | 
00214 |             {values.map((value, index) => {
00215 |               const x =
00216 |                 values.length === 1
00217 |                   ? 680 / 2
00218 |                   : 28 + ((680 - 56) * index) / Math.max(values.length - 1, 1);
00219 | 
00220 |               const y = 220 - 28 - ((220 - 56) * value) / Math.max(maxValue, 1);
00221 | 
00222 |               return (
00223 |                 <g key={`${labels[index]}-${value}`}>
00224 |                   <circle cx={x} cy={y} r="4" fill="#7C3AED" />
00225 |                   <text
00226 |                     x={x}
00227 |                     y={y - 10}
00228 |                     textAnchor="middle"
00229 |                     fontSize="11"
00230 |                     fill="#525252"
00231 |                   >
00232 |                     {value}
00233 |                   </text>
00234 |                 </g>
00235 |               );
00236 |             })}
00237 |           </svg>
00238 | 
00239 |           <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2 text-xs text-neutral-600">
00240 |             <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 md:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-6">
00241 |               {labels.map((label) => (
00242 |                 <div key={label} className="rounded-lg bg-neutral-50 px-2 py-2">
00243 |                   {label}
00244 |                 </div>
00245 |               ))}

00001 | import { cookies } from "next/headers";
00002 | 
00003 | import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase-admin";
00004 | 
00005 | export type OperationRole = "cashier" | "admin" | "superadmin";
00006 | 
00007 | export type OperationUser = {
00008 |   id: string;
00009 |   authUserId: string;
00010 |   displayName: string;
00011 |   role: OperationRole;
00012 | };
00013 | 
00014 | export type OperationSession =
00015 |   | {
00016 |       ok: true;
00017 |       role: OperationRole;
00018 |       userId: string;
00019 |       authUserId: string | null;
00020 |       displayName: string | null;
00021 |       source: "supabase";
00022 |     }
00023 |   | {
00024 |       ok: false;
00025 |       role: null;
00026 |       userId: null;
00027 |       authUserId: null;
00028 |       displayName: null;
00029 |       source: null;
00030 |     };
00031 | 
00032 | const OP_ACCESS_COOKIE = "nook_op_access_token";
00033 | 
00034 | export class OperationAuthUnavailableError extends Error {
00035 |   constructor(message = "Operational authentication temporarily unavailable") {
00036 |     super(message);
00037 |     this.name = "OperationAuthUnavailableError";
00038 |   }
00039 | }
00040 | 
00041 | function emptyOperationSession(): OperationSession {
00042 |   return {
00043 |     ok: false,
00044 |     role: null,
00045 |     userId: null,
00046 |     authUserId: null,
00047 |     displayName: null,
00048 |     source: null,
00049 |   };
00050 | }
00051 | 
00052 | function isOperationRole(value: unknown): value is OperationRole {
00053 |   return value === "cashier" || value === "admin" || value === "superadmin";
00054 | }
00055 | 
00056 | export async function getOperationalUserByAuthUserId(
00057 |   authUserId: string,
00058 | ): Promise<OperationUser | null> {
00059 |   const normalizedAuthUserId = String(authUserId || "").trim();
00060 | 
00061 |   if (!normalizedAuthUserId) {
00062 |     return null;
00063 |   }
00064 | 
00065 |   const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
00066 |     .from("operational_users")
00067 |     .select("id, auth_user_id, display_name, role, is_active")
00068 |     .eq("auth_user_id", normalizedAuthUserId)
00069 |     .maybeSingle();
00070 | 
00071 |   if (error) {
00072 |     console.error("AUTH_OPERATIONAL_USER_LOOKUP_ERROR", {
00073 |       code: error.code,
00074 |     });
00075 | 
00076 |     throw new OperationAuthUnavailableError();
00077 |   }
00078 | 
00079 |   if (!data) {
00080 |     console.warn("AUTH_OPERATIONAL_USER_NOT_FOUND");
00081 |     return null;
00082 |   }
00083 | 
00084 |   if (!data.is_active) {
00085 |     console.warn("AUTH_OPERATIONAL_USER_INACTIVE");
00086 |     return null;
00087 |   }
00088 | 
00089 |   if (!data.auth_user_id || !isOperationRole(data.role)) {
00090 |     console.warn("AUTH_OPERATIONAL_USER_INVALID");
00091 |     return null;
00092 |   }
00093 | 
00094 |   return {
00095 |     id: data.id,
00096 |     authUserId: data.auth_user_id,
00097 |     displayName: data.display_name,
00098 |     role: data.role,
00099 |   };
00100 | }
00101 | 
00102 | async function resolveSupabaseAuthUserId(): Promise<string | null> {
00103 |   const cookieStore = await cookies();
00104 |   const accessToken = cookieStore.get(OP_ACCESS_COOKIE)?.value;
00105 | 
00106 |   if (!accessToken) {
00107 |     console.warn("AUTH_ACCESS_COOKIE_MISSING");
00108 |     return null;
00109 |   }
00110 | 
00111 |   const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin.auth.getClaims(accessToken);
00112 | 
00113 |   if (error) {
00114 |     console.warn("AUTH_CLAIMS_REJECTED", {
00115 |       status: error.status,
00116 |       code: error.code,
00117 |     });
00118 |     return null;
00119 |   }
00120 | 
00121 |   const authUserId = data?.claims?.sub;
00122 | 
00123 |   if (!authUserId || typeof authUserId !== "string") {
00124 |     console.warn("AUTH_CLAIMS_SUB_MISSING");
00125 |     return null;
00126 |   }
00127 | 
00128 |   return authUserId;
00129 | }
00130 | 
00131 | async function getSupabaseOperationSession(): Promise<OperationSession> {
00132 |   const authUserId = await resolveSupabaseAuthUserId();
00133 | 
00134 |   if (!authUserId) {
00135 |     return emptyOperationSession();
00136 |   }
00137 | 
00138 |   const operationalUser = await getOperationalUserByAuthUserId(authUserId);
00139 | 
00140 |   if (!operationalUser) {
00141 |     return emptyOperationSession();
00142 |   }
00143 | 
00144 |   return {
00145 |     ok: true,
00146 |     role: operationalUser.role,
00147 |     userId: operationalUser.id,
00148 |     authUserId: operationalUser.authUserId,
00149 |     displayName: operationalUser.displayName,
00150 |     source: "supabase",
00151 |   };
00152 | }
00153 | 
00154 | export async function getOperationSession(): Promise<OperationSession> {
00155 |   return getSupabaseOperationSession();
00156 | }

00061 |   role: OperationRole,
00062 |   permission: OperationPermission,
00063 | ): boolean {

00065 | }

00068 |   | {

00071 |     }
00072 |   | {
00073 |       ok: false;
00074 |       status: 401 | 403;
00075 |       code: "UNAUTHENTICATED" | "FORBIDDEN";
00076 |       message: string;

00079 | export function authorizeOperationSession(
00080 |   session: OperationSession,
00081 |   permission: OperationPermission,
00082 | ): OperationAuthorizationResult {
00083 |   if (!session.ok) {
00084 |     return {
00085 |       ok: false,
00086 |       status: 401,
00087 |       code: "UNAUTHENTICATED",
00088 |       message: "No autenticado.",
00089 |     };
00090 |   }
00091 | 
00092 |   if (!hasOperationPermission(session.role, permission)) {
00093 |     return {
00094 |       ok: false,
00095 |       status: 403,
00096 |       code: "FORBIDDEN",
00097 |       message: "No tienes permisos para realizar esta acción.",
00098 |     };
00099 |   }
00100 | 
00101 |   return {
00102 |     ok: true,
00103 |     session,
00104 |   };
00105 | }

FILE: .\proxy.ts
============================================================
00001 | import { createClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
00002 | import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
00003 | 
00004 | const OP_ACCESS_COOKIE = "nook_op_access_token";
00005 | const OP_REFRESH_COOKIE = "nook_op_refresh_token";
00006 | 
00007 | function createAuthClient() {
00008 |   return createClient(
00009 |     process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
00010 |     process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
00011 |     {
00012 |       auth: {
00013 |         persistSession: false,
00014 |         autoRefreshToken: false,
00015 |         detectSessionInUrl: false,
00016 |       },
00017 |     },
00018 |   );
00019 | }
00020 | 
00021 | function setOperationalCookies(
00022 |   request: NextRequest,
00023 |   response: NextResponse,
00024 |   accessToken: string,
00025 |   refreshToken: string,
00026 |   expiresIn: number,
00027 | ) {
00028 |   request.cookies.set(OP_ACCESS_COOKIE, accessToken);
00029 |   request.cookies.set(OP_REFRESH_COOKIE, refreshToken);
00030 | 
00031 |   response.cookies.set(OP_ACCESS_COOKIE, accessToken, {
00032 |     httpOnly: true,
00033 |     secure: true,
00034 |     sameSite: "lax",
00035 |     path: "/",
00036 |     maxAge: expiresIn,
00037 |   });
00038 | 
00039 |   response.cookies.set(OP_REFRESH_COOKIE, refreshToken, {
00040 |     httpOnly: true,
00041 |     secure: true,
00042 |     sameSite: "lax",
00043 |     path: "/",
00044 |     maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 30,
00045 |   });
00046 | 
00047 |   response.headers.set("Cache-Control", "private, no-store");
00048 | }
00049 | 
00050 | async function refreshOperationalSession(
00051 |   request: NextRequest,
00052 | ): Promise<NextResponse | null> {
00053 |   const refreshToken = request.cookies.get(OP_REFRESH_COOKIE)?.value;
00054 | 
00055 |   if (!refreshToken) {
00056 |     return null;
00057 |   }
00058 | 
00059 |   const supabaseAuth = createAuthClient();
00060 | 
00061 |   const { data, error } = await supabaseAuth.auth.refreshSession({
00062 |     refresh_token: refreshToken,
00063 |   });
00064 | 
00065 |   if (error || !data.session || !data.user) {
00066 |     console.warn("AUTH_REFRESH_REJECTED", {
00067 |       status: error?.status,
00068 |       code: error?.code,
00069 |     });
00070 | 
00071 |     return null;
00072 |   }
00073 | 
00074 |   const response = NextResponse.next({
00075 |     request,
00076 |   });
00077 | 
00078 |   setOperationalCookies(
00079 |     request,
00080 |     response,
00081 |     data.session.access_token,
00082 |     data.session.refresh_token,
00083 |     data.session.expires_in,
00084 |   );
00085 | 
00086 |   console.info("AUTH_REFRESH_OK");
00087 | 
00088 |   return response;
00089 | }
00090 | 
00091 | export async function proxy(request: NextRequest) {
00092 |   const accessToken = request.cookies.get(OP_ACCESS_COOKIE)?.value;
00093 |   const refreshToken = request.cookies.get(OP_REFRESH_COOKIE)?.value;
00094 | 
00095 |   /*
00096 |    * Sesión normal:
00097 |    * no hacemos ninguna llamada adicional a Supabase.
00098 |    * La identidad y RBAC se validan donde corresponda mediante
00099 |    * getOperationSession().
00100 |    */
00101 |   if (accessToken) {
00102 |     return NextResponse.next();
00103 |   }
00104 | 
00105 |   /*
00106 |    * Sin access token y sin refresh token:
00107 |    * no existe una sesión operacional recuperable.
00108 |    */
00109 |   if (!refreshToken) {
00110 |     return NextResponse.next();
00111 |   }
00112 | 
00113 |   /*
00114 |    * El access token ya no está disponible, pero todavía existe
00115 |    * refresh token. Intentamos UNA renovación.
00116 |    */
00117 |   const refreshedResponse = await refreshOperationalSession(request);
00118 | 
00119 |   if (refreshedResponse) {
00120 |     return refreshedResponse;
00121 |   }
00122 | 
00123 |   /*
00124 |    * Un refresh fallido nunca concede acceso y tampoco destruye
00125 |    * las cookies. Las APIs continuarán rechazando una identidad
00126 |    * que no puedan verificar.
00127 |    */
00128 |   return NextResponse.next();
00129 | }
00130 | 
00131 | export const config = {
00132 |   matcher: [
00133 |     "/operacion/:path*",
00134 |     "/admin/:path*",
00135 |     "/api/operacion/:path*",
00136 |     "/api/admin/:path*",
00137 |     "/api/session",
00138 |     "/api/catalogo/:path*",
00139 |     "/api/subscriptions/:path*",
00140 |   ],
00141 | };
